# Fullseye — ViEW2026

把物理仿真与图像处理交给 AI 组合,并用真值验证。

**发表:2026 年 12 月 3 日(周四)14:40–15:50(日本时间),互动环节 2,IS2-19**(横滨 Pacifico)

点按图块即可打开视频或图(▶ = 动态)。

## 亲手试试

可以亲手操作论文中的图(手机上也能用)。用标签页切换方法,3-D 视图可拖动旋转。

- **[CAD 与实测点云的偏差检查](../viewers/poc_cad_scan_deviation.html?lang=zh)**: 配准会吸收缺陷:真值为 1.2 µm 的零件上出现了 121 µm 并不存在的凹坑。
- **[LiDAR 地形变化](../viewers/poc_lidar_terrain_change.html?lang=zh)**: 朴素相减会让没有变化的整个地块看起来下沉了 0.183 m。校正之后,只剩下真正的变化。
- **[模板跟踪](../viewers/poc_template_tracking.html?lang=zh)**: 即使已经切换到相似的另一个物体,跟踪器仍然报告“找到了”。
- **[脉冲热成像的深度估计](../viewers/poc_thermography_ndt.html?lang=zh)**: 只把拟合的时间窗从 25 秒缩到 4 秒,小缺陷深度的最大误差就从 +627% 缩小到 ±14%。问题出在窗口的选择,而不是物理。
- **[塔尔博特地毯与光栅 X 射线像](../viewers/poc_grating_interferometry.html?lang=zh)**: 在吸收像中裂纹的对比噪声比只有 1.34,在暗场像中升到 13.5,转动光栅还能读出纤维的方向。
- **[用条纹镜像看微米级凹坑](../viewers/poc_phase_measuring_deflectometry.html?lang=zh)**: 照片中的信号只有噪声的 0.17 倍,什么也看不见;而由条纹镜像做出的曲率图中,深 1 µm 的凹坑高出噪声 57 倍。
- **[小行星表面的弯曲程度](../viewers/poc_asteroid_curvature.html?lang=zh)**: 由真实小行星的点云算出曲率,用颜色看山脊和巨石边缘。先在答案已知的球面上验证了方法。
- **[景深合成](../viewers/poc_focus_stacking.html?lang=zh)**: 提高光圈值，景深合成图像越接近真实纹理
- **[白光干涉台阶](../viewers/poc_interferometry_step.html?lang=zh)**: 噪声增大时，白光干涉的台阶误差扩大
- **[偏振去镜面反射](../viewers/poc_polarization_specular.html?lang=zh)**: 偏振可去除镜面反光;残留部分随入射角变化,在布儒斯特角附近最小(扫描使用合成条纹场景)
- **[光弹性应力](../viewers/poc_photoelasticity.html?lang=zh)**: 转动检偏器会移动光弹性条纹，圆盘内的平均亮度随之变化（0–135° 范围）
- **[苍蝇复眼](../viewers/poc_compound_eye.html?lang=zh)**: 提高每视点信噪比，复眼图像越接近无噪声图像
- **[接合层空洞](../viewers/poc_ct_void_morphology.html?lang=zh)**: 提高阈值会扩大空洞分割并增加体积分数
- **[主动轮廓](../viewers/poc_active_contours.html?lang=zh)**: 迭代越多，GVF 轮廓越接近最终形状

With fewer bands the false alarms grew, so a band set that sufficed on synthetic data did not suffice on real data." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>用光谱图像找出与肉同色的异物</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thin_film_reflectometry/01_real_micrograph_thickness.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thin_film_reflectometry/01_real_micrograph_thickness.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thin_film_reflectometry/story.en.gif" alt="Oxide thickness on silicon can be read from interference fringes and colours in reflected light. It stays below the reference value, and the camera's colour sensitivity is unknown." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>从显微照片的颜色绘制氧化膜厚度图</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/08_real_alignment_plate.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/08_real_alignment_plate.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/story.en.gif" alt="Measured in one image, a stepped part looks larger where it is nearer, so an ordinary lens misreads its dimensions. On the published real images from a telecentric lens, the magnification matched the paper within 0.05 %. In the synthetic chapter, telecentric magnification barely moves with object height." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>用公开的远心实拍图像,将倍率与论文核对到 0.05 %</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/05_sem_same_part_3kv_3detectors.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/05_sem_same_part_3kv_3detectors.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/story.en.gif" alt="An SEM image is not a photo of the surface but a tally of electrons escaping after scattering inside the solid. A model that tracks each electron was checked against measured backscatter data for 7 elements. On the same line, the beam energy decides which sidewall height is read as the linewidth." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>虚拟 SEM:加速电压改变图像和线宽</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_cad_scan_deviation/14_align_orbit.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_cad_scan_deviation/01_scene.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_cad_scan_deviation/story.en.gif" alt="Aligning a scan to its design can absorb a defect or move it elsewhere. Known dents and warps were added to a part; even the defect-free control produced 66.5 square mm of false rejected area. Rigid-like deviations shrink, while nearest points jump across edges and create a fictitious dent." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>对齐会掩盖缺陷:CAD 与点云之差</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/04_visa_explanation.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/01_band_width_alignment.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/story.en.gif" alt="A classic inspection learns the usual per-pixel variation from good parts and flags what falls outside. Here the band is set by a false-alarm rate declared in advance and scored on 200 public real images. Alignment mattered more than the number of good parts, and the declared rate was not kept." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>用良品波动找缺陷:实拍图像上守不住宣称的误检率</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views/04_dual_energy.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views/01_slices.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views/story.en.gif" alt="Voids in a synthetic aluminium casting were scanned with cone-beam CT, rebuilt with FDK and iterative methods and scored on exact ellipsoids. FDK, last by image error, can still size voids better than the iterative method that ranks first. The best-looking reconstruction is not always the best measurement." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>铸件气孔 CT:RMSE 第一的重建,气孔体积未必第一</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/story.en.gif" alt="We gave an AI a task in natural language and let it assemble the inspection recipe. It was scored on 400 public crack images against the labels from their source, and each failure was sorted into tool, MCP or model. Cloud LLMs beat chance but did not reach the existing recipes." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>用真实数据的真值为 AI 组建的检测打分</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/01_checker_through_bottle.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/01_checker_through_bottle.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/story.en.gif" alt="A transparent bottle distorts the stripes behind it. For each pixel we find where the background is seen, fit the bottle geometry and indices backwards, and score 5 fill states against the true values of the simulation. Quantities that are inseparable and errors that stay undetectable are reported too." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>从背景条纹的畸变反推玻璃瓶(仿真):共同假设下的复原,以及无法分开或检测的量</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_tum_rgbd_slam/02_trajectory.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_tum_rgbd_slam/02_trajectory.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_tum_rgbd_slam/story.en.gif" alt="On public depth images of a desk, frame-to-frame alignment was checked against motion-capture truth. Error fell to 0.42 of no alignment, yet it was no better than assuming steady speed. Small-window plane residuals grew with distance, but quantisation and window size blur whether the law is a square." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>用公开的实测 RGB-D 给帧间配准打分:误差降到 0.42 倍,但与匀速假设大致相同</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/07_geometry_mistakes.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/07_geometry_mistakes.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/story.en.gif" alt="A walnut was really scanned in a cone beam, and the data were reconstructed with FDK and iterative methods. Using 300 projections and scoring against the reference from its authors, the result that ignores the vertical shift ends up much more distant. But the residual after reprojection cannot reveal the gap." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>实测 CT:忽略纵向偏移后离参考远 2.6 倍,残差却分辨不出</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_3d_sensor_modalities/01_map.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_3d_sensor_modalities/01_map.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_3d_sensor_modalities/story.en.gif" alt="The same part was measured by several depth-sensing methods in matte, glossy metal, translucent and black materials. <a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/02_equipment_at_a_glance.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/02_equipment_at_a_glance.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/story.en.gif" alt="We pre-tested visual inspection equipment on the same flaw. <a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_multiscanline/02_failure_map.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_multiscanline/02_failure_map.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_multiscanline/story.en.gif" alt="Using a real pair of stereo images, we compute disparity, distance, surfaces and even the pose of a circle. <a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_photoelasticity/03_load_and_isoclinics.mp4"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_photoelasticity/01_polariscope.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_photoelasticity/story.en.gif" alt="Viewed through polarisers, a loaded transparent plastic shows its internal stress as fringes. Most errors left after unwrapping came from the sign ambiguity, not the wrapping." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>受压圆盘的应力以条纹显现(光弹性)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/08_depal_robot_video.mp4"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/01_overview_same_pattern.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/story.en.gif" alt="Packed cartons with identical prints are told apart from overhead depth and colour, then unloaded. With identical prints, deep grooves favour colour; only wide, untaped cuts over shallow grooves favour depth." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>用深度台阶、V 形缝或彩色分开混载托盘上的纸箱</span></a>

A single-feature outlier check rejected unseen pits more often than the learned density model did." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>用训练的分类器分拣金属表面缺陷:沿磨削纹的划痕四条漏三条,未见过的蚀坑拒识 95 %</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/05_highlight_sweep.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/01_breakdown_occlusion.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/story.en.gif" alt="Normalised cross-correlation, the usual template matcher, fails when part of the target is hidden or glare washes it out. They withstand occlusion far better, yet every method stays weak to rotation." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>耐过曝与遮挡的符号匹配</span></a>

If transport and capture drift out of sync, the stacked image smears." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>在暗的产线上叠加 TDI 级数,浅伤浮现</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_itof_multifreq/12_public_raw_unwrap.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_itof_multifreq/01_warehouse_unwrap.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_itof_multifreq/story.en.gif" alt="An indirect ToF camera measures distance from the phase delay of modulated light. <a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/02_map_detections.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/02_map_detections.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/story.en.gif" alt="Miscounting pores in a weld radiograph leads to the wrong weld grade. Estimating the background before detection keeps only real pores, and far fewer images are graded wrongly." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>焊缝 X 射线像的气孔:固定阈值连余高边缘也算进去</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/01_usaf_4x_fp_20x.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/01_usaf_4x_fp_20x.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/story.en.gif" alt="A low-magnification objective sees a wide field but misses fine detail. Combining images lit from different angles in frequency space resolved detail as fine as a 20x image over the same wide field. When the illumination overlap is too small to converge, no answer is returned." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>保持 4 倍视野得到 20 倍分辨率(傅里叶叠层成像)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/02_works_by_cone.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/02_works_by_cone.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/story.en.gif" alt="On near-mirror metal parts, shallow dents and scratches on a hairline finish show or vanish with the width of the cone of light more than with the lamp type. With a cone half-angle of 4°, the hairline streaks disappear and only the scratch stays dark. A single dent can appear or disappear as the cone opens." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>照明光锥的张角决定凹痕和划伤是否可见</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_simcal_telecentric/02_real_model_residuals.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_simcal_telecentric/02_real_model_residuals.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_simcal_telecentric/story.en.gif" alt="An imaging model was fitted to a published real image of a tilted alignment plate seen through a telecentric lens. The magnification came out at 252.14 px/mm, close to the paper's value. Places where the model does not fit are kept and drawn as residual needles on the image." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>用一张远心实拍图像标定,不吻合处也标出</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_lidar_terrain_change/13_real_dod.png"><img src="../thumbs/poc_lidar_terrain_change.jpg" alt="两期航空 LiDAR:不减去系统差,整片都像被挖掉" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><span>两期航空 LiDAR:不减去系统差,整片都像被挖掉</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_depth/01_scene.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_depth/01_scene.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_depth/story.en.gif" alt="A public real stereo photograph with true disparity is used to check depth measurement. Setting the disparity search below the true maximum loses the whole foreground, with 95.06 % of pixels wrong. When converting to distance, ignoring a single calibration value bends the shape." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>实拍立体:视差搜索范围不够,前景全错</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sem_tem_same_particle/08_tem_focus_sweep.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sem_tem_same_particle/02_same_specimen_sem_tem.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sem_tem_same_particle/story.en.gif" alt="The same nanoparticles are sized in both a scanning and a transmission electron image. Even for a 52 nm particle, the reading moved by nanometres with the imaging mode and cut-off level. Scored against a shared truth, both biases follow simple formulas for edge blur and defocus." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>同一颗粒,SEM 与 TEM 的粒径相差数 nm</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tem_ctf_thon_rings/12_real_empiar_10028.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tem_ctf_thon_rings/02_thon_rings_four_defoci.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tem_ctf_thon_rings/story.en.gif" alt="In a transmission electron microscope, defocus decides whether fine detail looks bright or dark. Estimating defocus from 3 public real micrographs closely matched the authors' own software. Sweeping focus moves the concentric Thon rings and flips detail near true focus." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>从真实电镜像推定离焦,与作者值相差 0.2 % 以内</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_optics_drawings/06_setup_ring_coaxial.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_optics_drawings/06_setup_ring_coaxial.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_optics_drawings/story.en.gif" alt="In meetings about inspection systems, the lens layout and the setup drawing are the common language. The optical calculation now draws them directly, up to ISO 10110 element drawings. Each written file is read back, and its lines and text are scored against the calculation." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>从光学计算直接画出装置布置图和透镜图纸</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_crack_width_timeseries/11_series_video.gif"><img src="../thumbs/poc_crack_width_timeseries.jpg" alt="裂缝扩展:二值化计数把速度高估到 2.5 倍" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><b>&#9654;</b><span>裂缝扩展:二值化计数把速度高估到 2.5 倍</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_moire_alignment/01_marks.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_moire_alignment/02_one_nm_steps.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_moire_alignment/story.en.gif" alt="Lithography must overlay a mask and a wafer to within nanometres. Overlaying two gratings too fine for the camera to see produces 102 µm fringes that magnify the shift. Reading the fringe position recovers shifts smaller than a nanometre." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>叠加两片看不见的光栅,用莫尔条纹读出 nm 级偏移</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor/01_line.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor/01_line.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor/story.en.gif" alt="When a laser line measures height, speckle, the grainy pattern of interfering light, breaks up the line. Reading position by the centroid still leaves an error of 6.4 µm, close to the floor the formula predicts. Averaging frames of a static part does not help; only moving the part lowers it." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>激光光切的颗粒(散斑)决定高度精度的下限</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/02_frames.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/02_frames.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/story.en.gif" alt="When a laser line traces a weld bead, a steeper camera angle measures height more finely. Even at 28 degrees, part of the undercut groove falls into its own shadow and its depth reads shallow. The shadowed span still looks measured, which makes the loss easy to miss." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>焊道光切:角度变大,咬边被自身阴影遮住</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_am_thermal_to_ct/07_ct_hole_crown.png"><img src="../thumbs/poc_am_thermal_to_ct.jpg" alt="用真实 CT 测量金属增材件朝下表面的粗糙" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><span>用真实 CT 测量金属增材件朝下表面的粗糙</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thermography_ndt/07_window_explained.mp4"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thermography_ndt/01_depth_table.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thermography_ndt/story.en.gif" alt="A flash warms the surface, and the cooling thermal images reveal how deep a hidden delamination lies. For a small, shallow defect, cutting the fitting window to 4 s restored a correct depth. The error came not from heat spreading sideways but from the choice of window." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>用热像测剥离深度:答案随时间窗改变</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_interferometry_step/09_csi_scan_explained.mp4"><img src="../thumbs/poc_interferometry_step.jpg" alt="用白光干涉测纳米级台阶" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><b>&#9654;</b><span>用白光干涉测纳米级台阶</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_glyph_typo_detection/01_sign_before_after.png"><img src="../thumbs/poc_glyph_typo_detection.jpg" alt="不识别文字也能找出并改正标牌的错字" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><span>不识别文字也能找出并改正标牌的错字</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weed_rice_ratio/05_real_examples.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weed_rice_ratio/05_real_examples.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weed_rice_ratio/story.en.gif" alt="Aerial photos of rice paddies are graded by how much weedy rice is mixed into the crop. ## 能做什么

共 101 项,每项都有说明页(用法与限制)和可运行的示例。

<div class="vl" markdown="1">

**查找**

- 切出区域、筛选并计数: [说明](../../capabilities/blob-and-region.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_cell_counting](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_cell_counting.py) · [poc_particle_sizing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_particle_sizing.py) · [poc_real_coin_metrology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_coin_metrology.py) · [poc_dense_bubble_count](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dense_bubble_count.py)
- 从田间 RGB 用颜色与纹理区分作物和杂草,并按事先声明的边界给杂草覆盖度分级(不用学习器): [说明](../../capabilities/crop-weed-cover-grade.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_weed_rice_ratio](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_weed_rice_ratio.py)
- 用两种 X 射线能量分离厚度与材质(双能 / 光子计数: 能谱 → 能量窗 → 基分解 → 有效原子序数): [说明](../../capabilities/dual-energy-xray-material-discrimination.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_dual_energy_xray](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dual_energy_xray.py)
- 按应有的字符串修正图像中的文字: [说明](../../capabilities/fix-text-in-images.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [fix_text_in_image](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/fix_text_in_image.py) · [poc_glyph_typo_detection](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_glyph_typo_detection.py)
- 排列良品来判断“与平时不同”(良品统计变动模型,附声明的误检率): [说明](../../capabilities/good-sample-variation-model.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_good_sample_variation_model](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_good_sample_variation_model.py) · [anomaly_eval_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/anomaly_eval_metrics.py)
- OCR 预处理(倾斜校正、局部二值化、行切分): [说明](../../capabilities/ocr-preprocessing.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [gallery2d_segmentation](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_segmentation.py) · [poc_matrix_code_reading](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_matrix_code_reading.py) · [gallery2d_region](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_region.py)
- 仅从良品学习并指出偏离的图块(经典特征的逐位置正态分布与记忆库,保证召回率的工作点): [说明](../../capabilities/patch-feature-anomaly-detection.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_patch_anomaly](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_patch_anomaly.py) · [anomaly_eval_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/anomaly_eval_metrics.py)
- 用载流子扩散 PSF 和缺陷类型区分 PL 像中细沟槽消失的原因（Si 晶圆·太阳能电池的 PL/EL 检测）: [说明](../../capabilities/pl-carrier-diffusion-visibility.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_si_wafer_pl_defect_visibility](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_si_wafer_pl_defect_visibility.py)
- 找到小的点状目标并以亚像素定位: [说明](../../capabilities/point-target-detection.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_search_sweep_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_search_sweep_width.py) · [poc_astro_photometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_astro_photometry.py)
- 耐光照不均、高光与遮挡的模板匹配(把亮度变化的方向作为符号比较,无法定位的模板则拒绝): [说明](../../capabilities/robust-code-matching.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_code_matching_breakdown](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_code_matching_breakdown.py)
- 用超声回波的相位而不是亮度找出混合键合中的空洞(扫描声学显微镜的 C 扫描、频率权衡、检出概率): [说明](../../capabilities/sam-bond-voids.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids.py)
- 从点云中以六自由度定位已知三维零件: [说明](../../capabilities/six-dof-surface-pose.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_registration_basin](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_registration_basin.py) · [poc_real_tless_6dof](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_tless_6dof.py)
- 表面凹凸缺陷:从照明设计到检测: [说明](../../capabilities/surface-defects-with-lighting.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [illumination_design_demo](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/illumination_design_demo.py) · [photometric_stereo](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/photometric_stereo.py) · [poc_bump_coplanarity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bump_coplanarity.py) · [poc_surface_defect_classifiers](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_surface_defect_classifiers.py)
- 定位(用模板匹配让测量线跟随): [说明](../../capabilities/template-alignment.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_template_tracking](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_template_tracking.py) · [gallery2d_contour_measure](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_contour_measure.py) · [poc_search_sweep_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_search_sweep_width.py)

**测量**

- 从平面标定板的多视图估计内参矩阵 K(张正友法): [说明](../../capabilities/camera-intrinsics-calibration.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [camera_intrinsics_calibration](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/camera_intrinsics_calibration.py) · [poc_caltab_chain_truth](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_caltab_chain_truth.py)
- 把复平面当作面来看(域着色、吸引域、逃逸时间、绕翼流动): [说明](../../capabilities/complex-plane-fields.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_complex_plane_fields](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_complex_plane_fields.py)
- 以闭式求周期图案在采样晶格上混叠的距离，以及条纹所扰乱的碰撞时间（复眼与检测相机）: [说明](../../capabilities/compound-eye-aliasing-and-ttc.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_zebra_stripes_fly_eye](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_zebra_stripes_fly_eye.py)
- 用 EMVA 1288 的步骤重新测量相机规格书上的数值,并看相机内部的预处理会使这些数值偏移多少(Linear 与 General): [说明](../../capabilities/emva1288-camera-datasheets.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_emva1288_sensor](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_emva1288_sensor.py) · [poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing.py)
- 从本应笔直的线估计畸变系数(铅垂线法,无需棋盘格): [说明](../../capabilities/estimate-lens-distortion.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [estimate_lens_distortion](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/estimate_lens_distortion.py)
- 傅里叶叠层成像(LED 斜向照明的图像组 → 相位恢复得到高 NA 的振幅与相位,不收敛则拒绝): [说明](../../capabilities/fourier-ptychography.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_fourier_ptychography](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_fourier_ptychography.py)

- 从振动与声音诊断异常: [说明](../../capabilities/vibration-and-acoustics.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_bearing_diagnosis](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bearing_diagnosis.py) · [poc_rail_corrugation](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_rail_corrugation.py)

**重建与校正**

- 用同一几何描述重建锥束、螺旋和分层扫描(工业 CT): [说明](../../capabilities/ct-scan-modes.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [ct_scan_modes](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/ct_scan_modes.py)
- 对整幅图像校正镜头畸变(桶形、枕形、切向): [说明](../../capabilities/lens-distortion-correction.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [lens_undistort](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/lens_undistort.py)
- 从投影重建断面(CT): [说明](../../capabilities/tomography-reconstruction.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_ct_fidelity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_ct_fidelity.py) · [poc_ct_void_morphology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_ct_void_morphology.py)
- 从剪影雕出立体(视觉外壳): [说明](../../capabilities/visual-hull-from-silhouettes.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [space_carving](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/space_carving.py) · [poc_livestock_body_volume](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_livestock_body_volume.py)

**搭建流程**

- 对齐并叠加: [说明](../../capabilities/align-and-stack.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_astro_photometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_astro_photometry.py) · [poc_registration_basin](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_registration_basin.py)
- 把图像传给点、点云、体积、网格和图,再传回来: [说明](../../capabilities/convert-between-representations.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_bga_ball_type_roundtrip](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bga_ball_type_roundtrip.py) · [representation_roundtrip](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/representation_roundtrip.py) · [representation_conversion](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/representation_conversion.py) · [type_connectivity_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/type_connectivity_tour.py)
- 与基准图像(黄金样本)比较、测缺陷、按批判定: [说明](../../capabilities/golden-compare.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [golden_compare](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/golden_compare.py)
- 用已知良品/不良品集在部署前检定检测配方与规格,并测量余量: [说明](../../capabilities/inspection-fixture.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [inspection_fixture](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/inspection_fixture.py)
- 一次调用检测整个文件夹(批处理、按规格判定、汇总、SPC、报告、审计日志): [说明](../../capabilities/inspection-workflow.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [inspection_workflow](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/inspection_workflow.py)
- 以 MTF 与良率分配镜头制造公差: [说明](../../capabilities/lens-tolerancing.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_lens_tolerancing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lens_tolerancing.py)
- 同一输入的验证、邻域搜索与变换核只计算一次: [说明](../../capabilities/reuse-computation-results.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [perf_pointcloud_descriptors](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_pointcloud_descriptors.py) · [perf_monogenic_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_monogenic_metrics.py) · [perf_prepared_volume_probe](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_prepared_volume_probe.py) · [perf_polarization_analyze](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_polarization_analyze.py) · [perf_hankel_plan_and_piv_stream](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_hankel_plan_and_piv_stream.py)
- 读取科学数据文件(FITS、NIfTI、MRC、OME-TIFF、E57): [说明](../../capabilities/scientific-data-files.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_real_file_formats](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_file_formats.py)
- 把 op 的返回值(带类型)写成 JSON,并逐位还原: [说明](../../capabilities/typed-results-as-json.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [typed_results_json](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/typed_results_json.py)
- 把 op 的返回值(带类型)做成可读的 Markdown,并内嵌 JSON 以便还原: [说明](../../capabilities/typed-results-as-markdown.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [typed_results_markdown](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/typed_results_markdown.py)
- 把带类型的检测结果写入 Excel(.xlsx)报告: [说明](../../capabilities/xlsx-report.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [xlsx_report](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/xlsx_report.py)

**呈现**

- 把结果做成人能读懂的图: [说明](../../capabilities/figures-and-annotation.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_colormap_readability](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_colormap_readability.py) · [poc_dem_terrain](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dem_terrain.py)

- 把点云原样画成彩色图像(亚像素足迹、按深度合成、保持光总量的叠加): [说明](../../capabilities/point-cloud-to-picture.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [points_render_basics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/points_render_basics.py) · [poc_flybrain_pointcloud](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_flybrain_pointcloud.py) · [poc_real_tless_6dof](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_tless_6dof.py)
- 把文字和表格放到图像上想要的位置: [说明](../../capabilities/text-and-tables-on-images.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [annotate_paper_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/annotate_paper_tour.py)

**绘制**

- 把照片变成一条线(点描 → 巡回路径 → 旋转的圆): [说明](../../capabilities/one-stroke-drawing.md) _(ja)_ · 示例 [poc_one_stroke_epicycles](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_one_stroke_epicycles.py)

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_veiling_glare/01_verdict.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_veiling_glare/01_verdict.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_veiling_glare/story.en.gif" alt="Veiling Glare Breaks Contrast Measurement — MTF (modulation transfer function) Passes While Black Level Fails" loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>杂散光</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_emva1288_sensor/01_raw_dark_flat_saturation.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_emva1288_sensor/01_raw_dark_flat_saturation.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_emva1288_sensor/story.en.gif" alt="Measuring a Camera Without Buying One — Recovering Quantum Efficiency, Gain and Dark Noise from a Planted Sensor with the EMVA 1288 (image-sensor characterization standard) Procedure" loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>EMVA 1288 测量</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing/01_datasheet_lies.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing/01_datasheet_lies.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing/story.en.gif" alt="In-Camera Processing Makes Datasheet Numbers Lie Plausibly - Denoising Adds 83 % to the Quantum Efficiency, the Standard's Check Misses It, Noise Correlation Catches It" loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>相机内处理让规格书数值失真</span></a>

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_profile/05_real_laser_line.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_profile/05_real_laser_line.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_profile/story.en.gif" alt="A weld bead profile is read from the rows of a laser line. Refusing to report unmeasured quantities stops that error, but the depth bias from speckle remains." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>焊道截面</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/02_frames.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/02_frames.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/story.en.gif" alt="When a laser line traces a weld bead, a steeper camera angle measures height more finely. Even at 28 degrees, part of the undercut groove falls into its own shadow and its depth reads shallow. The shadowed span still looks measured, which makes the loss easy to miss." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>焊道扫描</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/02_map_detections.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/02_map_detections.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/story.en.gif" alt="Miscounting pores in a weld radiograph leads to the wrong weld grade. A fixed cut-off also counts the edge of the weld cap and reports 124 blobs. Estimating the background before detection keeps only real pores, and far fewer images are graded wrongly." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>焊缝气孔</span></a>

A single dent can appear or disappear as the cone opens." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>照明光锥与观察 NA</span></a>

If transport and capture drift out of sync, the stacked image smears." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>TDI 线扫描相机与线扫描镜头</span></a>

Layers too thin to separate are reported as unmeasurable instead of guessed." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>工业 OCT(涂层厚度与内部缺陷)</span></a>

When the illumination overlap is too small to converge, no answer is returned." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>傅里叶叠层成像(4 倍视野中的 20 倍分辨率)</span></a>

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dual_energy_xray/13_real_tray_xray.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dual_energy_xray/13_real_tray_xray.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dual_energy_xray/story.en.gif" alt="In a food X-ray image, thick meat and a thin hard fragment can look the same. <a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_patch_anomaly/01_anomaly_maps.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_patch_anomaly/01_anomaly_maps.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_patch_anomaly/story.en.gif" alt="Anomaly Detection from Good Samples Only — Classical Patch Features, a Per-Position Distribution Against a Memory Bank on One Yard-Stick, Down to a Recall-Guaranteeing Threshold" loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>仅从良品学习的图块特征异常检测(经典特征的分布与记忆)</span></a>

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/08_depal_robot_video.mp4"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/01_overview_same_pattern.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/story.en.gif" alt="Packed cartons with identical prints are told apart from overhead depth and colour, then unloaded. A depth step splits none, and whether depth dips or dark colour lines win flips across 28 flap-seam builds. With identical prints, deep grooves favour colour; only wide, untaped cuts over shallow grooves favour depth." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>用深度台阶、V 形缝或彩色分开混载托盘上的纸箱</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/story.en.gif" alt="We gave an AI a task in natural language and let it assemble the inspection recipe. It was scored on 400 public crack images against the labels from their source, and each failure was sorted into tool, MCP or model. Cloud LLMs beat chance but did not reach the existing recipes." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>用真实数据的真值为 AI 组建的检测打分</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/02_equipment_at_a_glance.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/02_equipment_at_a_glance.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/story.en.gif" alt="We pre-tested visual inspection equipment on the same flaw. With 60 combinations of lighting, camera and lens we mapped how small a flaw is found, with the spread across repeats. It is a synthetic test, not the performance of real equipment." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>器材选型地图:用 60 种照明×相机×镜头组合拍同一缺陷</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views/01_slices.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views/01_slices.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views/story.en.gif" alt="Voids in a synthetic aluminium casting were scanned with cone-beam CT, rebuilt with FDK and iterative methods and scored on exact ellipsoids. FDK, last by image error, can still size voids better than the iterative method that ranks first. The best-looking reconstruction is not always the best measurement." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>铸件气孔 CT:RMSE 第一的重建,气孔体积未必第一</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/01_defect_patches.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/01_defect_patches.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/story.en.gif" alt="Synthetic ground-metal patches carry scratches, dents and stains, and learned models sort them. Only 0.324 of the scratches lying along the lay were found, and the cause is not identified. A single-feature outlier check rejected unseen pits more often than the learned density model did." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>用训练的分类器分拣金属表面缺陷:沿磨削纹的划痕四条漏三条,未见过的蚀坑拒识 95 %</span></a>
</div>
</details>

<details class="vall"><summary><b>尺寸与形状测量</b> (60)</summary>
<div class="vg vs">
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_aoi_ct_traceability/01_inspection_and_reconstructed_ct.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_aoi_ct_traceability/01_inspection_and_reconstructed_ct.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_aoi_ct_traceability/story.en.gif" alt="Full visual inspection and sampled CT must assign the same index to each joint. Unequal-sided fiducials fixed this correspondence, then top-ranked candidates caught 19 % of interface-touching voids. Apparent void rankings can agree while most life-limiting parts are missed." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>AOI 与 CT 对应</span></a>

García, CVIU 152, 2016; GTI, Universidad Politécnica de Madrid)(来源 https://www.gti.ssr.upm.es/data/lasiesta_database),采用 CC BY-SA 4.0(https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/),不适用 Apache-2.0。修改内容见 [NOTICE](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/NOTICE)。 [CC BY-SA 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/) · [LASIESTA](https://www.gti.ssr.upm.es/data/lasiesta_database)

## 能看到什么/测得的数字

所有数字都是相对各 PoC 自己植入的真值(闭式、解析解或公开值)的实测;运行 PoC 会打印出相同的值。

<div class="vl" markdown="1">

**图像检测与外观测量**

- [用公开的远心实拍图像,将倍率与论文核对到 0.05 %](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/08_real_alignment_plate.png) (图): Measured in one image, a stepped part looks larger where it is nearer, so an ordinary lens misreads its dimensions. On the published real images from a telecentric lens, the magnification matched the paper within 0.05 %. In the synthetic chapter, telecentric magnification barely moves with object height.
  The lead chapter measures the paper's raw telecentric images (Stark et al., Sci. Rep. 15, 28690, 2025, CC BY 4.0) with Fullseye's metrology ops and checks them against the paper: a family of ellipses sharing one centre and shape, fitted to the eight rings of an alignment plate (1 mm spacing) tilted by 29.5°, gives 252.13 ± 0.04 px/mm along the major axis, +0.05 % from the paper's K1 = 252.0 px/mm (the paper's own K1 and K2 spread by ±0.25 %, so the match is not read as finer than that). The telecentric signature: the centres of the concentric rings on the tilted plate do not drift with r² (β = 0.0035 ± 0.0034 px/mm², where a 25 mm entocentric lens would give 1.40 and move the outer centre by 101 px), so the entrance pupil lies beyond 10.5 m. Fitting a free ellipse to each ring instead fails on the outer rings whose arcs are shadowed down to 36–180°. A 3D-printed resin sphere (nominal radius 2.5 mm) is not a usable reference: its outline radius moves 8 % with the threshold. The synthetic chapters that follow sweep the failure conditions. Die bonding and connector-pin inspection measure features at different heights in one image. An ordinary (entocentric) lens magnifies nearer faces more, so the same dimension reads differently above and below a step; a telecentric lens puts the stop at a focal point so the chief rays run parallel to the axis. Seven new ops (`telecentric`, category `telecentric` of `opsoptics`): the same BK7-class singlet (f = 100 mm) with only the stop moved (entocentric, object-side and bi-telecentric), the real chief ray found by aiming at the stop itself so it works with the entrance pupil at infinity (where `raytrace.chief_ray` stops), magnification versus height, the error across a step, defocus blur, vignetting and a pinhole camera at the entrance pupil for `render3d`. Moving the object 5 mm closer on a fixed image plane changes the magnification by +6.9 % for a 25 mm machine-vision lens at 75 mm, +1.7 % for the 100 mm entocentric lens and −0.013 % for the telecentric ones; the real rays agree with L/(L − Δz) to 7e-5, and refocusing does not help (m = −f/(s − f) moves by +2.6 %). Rendered through the three lenses, a 5 mm pin top reads 6.9 % too large with the short lens (closed form to 7e-4) and its reading moves 8.6 % over a 6 mm stroke; 2,053 pixels of the bore wall show and only 0.948 of the hole bottom is visible (circle-overlap closed form 0.949), while the bi-telecentric view sees the whole bottom and no wall. The parts are rendered with real material appearance (a brushed aluminium fixture plate, a gold-plated pin, a black moulded package with tinned leads; machining marks and metal colours from complex refractive indices, a clear-coat resin, shadows and ambient occlusion), and under coaxial, dome and low-angle ring lighting a circle metrology model applied to the rendered image reads the pin top within 0.33 % of the closed form, +8.3 to 8.4 % across a 6 mm stroke for the short lens and within ±0.16 % for the bi-telecentric one; at a coarser 47 µm per pixel the low-angle ring reading drifts by 2 % because the dark-field edge is weak. What remains: the singlet's pupil spherical aberration leaves 0.05° at 8 mm field (growing as h³) and 3 % distortion for the object-side design (0.005 % bi-telecentric); a stop shift moves the entrance pupil to −f²/δ as Newton's equation says (0.04 % at 0.5 mm field, 11.8 % more at 8 mm); a residual 0.1° shifts a point 9.2 µm across a 5 mm step (closed form 8.7 µm). The front lens sets the field: the 50 % point at 11.6 mm falls 0.8 mm short of the paraxial overlap because pupil aberration shifts and widens the bundle. The blur slope matches |m|·NA/√2 within 0.5 %, and NA 0.033 (the smallest that resolves 22 µm) caps the depth of field at 0.68 mm.  **A second implementation, the open-source optiland, gives the same chief-ray image height to 9e-8 mm (with the same index from a public glass catalogue, so it checks the tracing and aiming code). Nothing learned, nothing calibrated, 29 gates.** [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_telecentric_metrology.py)
- [用良品波动找缺陷:实拍图像上守不住宣称的误检率](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/04_visa_explanation.png) (图): A classic inspection learns the usual per-pixel variation from good parts and flags what falls outside. Here the band is set by a false-alarm rate declared in advance and scored on 200 public real images. Alignment mattered more than the number of good parts, and the declared rate was not kept.
  The inspection classic, learning every pixel's usual value and spread from many good samples and flagging what leaves the band, as an op family whose band width is derived from a declared per-pixel false-alarm rate instead of being set by hand (five new varmodel_* ops plus four method-independent anomeval metrics). For normal good samples a new pixel's deviation follows a t prediction interval, so the band width is closed-form. False-alarm rates counted on synthetic good samples fall inside the binomial 4-sigma band for per-pixel std, one pooled variance, empirical-Bayes shrinkage (Smyth 2004) and median/MAD, and the pooled variance fails when the noise differs per pixel. With five good samples the std band is 9.4 sigma wide (the t4 tail); shrinkage brings it to 5.0 and lifts the detection of 5-sigma flaws from 0.13 to 0.52. A good set shifted by 1-2 px is refused; forced through, the band on edges grows 22-fold and misses every edge flaw, and registering by a 1/20-px Fourier shift brings it back to 1.07. With three of twenty "good" samples carrying the same flaw, std misses the real flaw there (0.00) while median/MAD finds it (0.98). On the public VisA pcb1 data (CC BY 4.0, 200 test images) the registered band reaches pixel AUROC 0.808 and AUPRO 0.678 against 0.705 and 0.460 unregistered; golden comparison scores a higher pixel AUROC (0.826) but a lower AUPRO (0.519).  **The declared 1e-3 false-alarm rate is not kept on real images (3.8 %), and recalibrating on held-out good images only reaches 2.4 %: a difference between the training and test captures remains that a translation cannot remove. A stochastic texture (DAGM 2007, CC BY 4.0) has no structure in its mean image, its alignment is reported as unobservable and detection is at chance (pixel AUROC 0.465): where the method works and where it does not, both in numbers.** [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_good_sample_variation_model.py)
- [铸件气孔 CT:RMSE 第一的重建,气孔体积未必第一](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views/04_dual_energy.png) (图): Voids in a synthetic aluminium casting were scanned with cone-beam CT, rebuilt by several methods, and scored on exact ellipsoids. With 12 views, the classic method that ranks last by image error sized the voids better than the best iterative one. The best-looking reconstruction is not always the best measurement.
  Three ellipsoidal voids (24 to 84 voxels) sit in an aluminium casting (32³ at 0.25 mm), scanned by cone-beam CT under three conditions. The ground truth is the analytic ellipsoids themselves: the drawn void volumes match 4/3πabc within 0.2 %, the discrete projector matches the closed-form projections to 4.0 % relative L2 (2.6 % at 64³), and the projector pair is adjoint to 3.8e-16. The data are closed-form projections plus noise, not the discrete projector the reconstructions use. By RMSE, 36 views over 360° rank OS-SART ≈ SIRT 0.031 < TV 0.032 < FDK 0.053, while at 12 views and over 110° TV moves to first (0.77 and 0.78 of the better algebraic method). Yet at 12 views FDK, last by RMSE, measures void volume from the attenuation deficit within 0.9 % of the closed form, better than TV at 5.9 %. Over 110° FDK finds none of the voids. Thresholding at half the aluminium value biases even the true volume by 16.7 %, so any method that looks better than that is flagged as cancelling biases. The TV weight 3e-3 was chosen on a separate calibration casting; a five-point hindsight sweep on the evaluation casting agrees over 110° but prefers 1e-2 at 12 views (2.4 % lower RMSE), and TV's deficit-based void-volume error moves from 2.3 % to 59 % with the weight (so FDK beating TV holds for this way of picking the weight). Redrawing the noise with four other seeds does not flip any ranking. Dual energy measures the steel insert at −8.7 % and pays with 41.65 times the noise in the aluminium basis (closed form 41.00). Monochromatic, Gaussian noise, convex voids and illustrative attenuation coefficients. [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_ct_casting_sparse_views.py)
  Terms: cone-beam CT — A CT geometry in which a point source illuminates a flat-panel detector with a cone of X-rays, so one rotation records a whole volume.
- [用真实数据的真值为 AI 组建的检测打分](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png) (图): We gave an AI a task in natural language and let it assemble the inspection recipe. It was scored on 400 public crack images against the labels from their source, and each failure was sorted into tool, MCP or model. Cloud LLMs beat chance but did not reach the existing recipes.
  A task is given in natural language and an LLM uses only Fullseye's MCP server (minimal profile) to find operators (index search), check the type chain and, as instructed, try candidates on six development images before returning a recipe (an operator chain), scored against the ground truth of public real data. Only a local generator script calls the LLM; every recipe, including runs that returned none, is saved as JSON with a pinned fingerprint and replayed deterministically through the same entry point as MCP. Tasks: cracks in concrete walls (Özgenel 2019, CC BY 4.0; 400 test images with the provider's labels) and the VisA candle set (CC BY 4.0; 197 test images with pixel masks). A recipe 'sorts' when the lower end of the 95% interval of its balanced accuracy is above 0.5 and it does not flag 95% or more of the images; this is beating chance, not a usable inspection. How the runs were redone (found by a review independent of the author): batch 1 sent no context length and the server default of 4,096 tokens very likely dropped the task text. The development images were read in 1 of 40 runs in batch 1, 40 of 40 in batch 2 (context 32,768) and 39 of 40 in batch 3, after fixing the MCP server (task files listed, missing names refused with readable names, out-of-range knobs rejected by the validator, a fix for knobs inside params, crack and stain queries reaching bothat and hx_plane_deviation). Results (local 14B and 32B code-tuned models, ten runs each per task): recipes that sort cracks: 1, 3 and 1 of 20 in batches 1-3 (0 to 2 per model and batch), so the local models rarely succeed and do not repeat it. The best of them on cracks, a batch-2 14B recipe (0.995 detected, 0.45 false alarms, balanced accuracy 0.773), beats every cloud run; none sort candles in any batch. Most batch 2 and 3 recipes were edge detectors flagging 95% or more of the images (26 of 40 and 31 of 40); without the rule that excludes them, batch-3 crack counts would be 32B 1/10, 14B 4/10. The MCP fixes did not raise success, and from batch 2 on almost every failure is attributed to the model (batch 1 failures were all attributed to the tool; the MCP rule alone would claim 23 of its 39 failures). A cloud LLM agent (separate column, MCP before the fixes) had the same MCP, task text, system prompt, no pixels and a 24-call cap (the local tool counted calls only at the start of a turn and ran up to 33; the 10 runs over the cap are scored with the recipe cut at call 24). Not matched: the local runs get one nudge each for answering without a trial or without JSON, the cloud model's product name is deliberately not written and its temperature is not exposed by the general-purpose coding agent that ran it, and all six cloud runs cut or parsed the printed responses with their own commands (a breach of the brief; the local models read the whole truncated response). All three cloud runs sort cracks, but with 0.32-0.52 false alarms, i.e. only above chance (best 0.81 detected, 0.32 false alarms); one of three sorts candles (0.26, 0.12). References: on cracks, an existing recipe (the hairline-scratch recipe applied unchanged) detects 0.69 with 0.01 false alarms, above the best cloud run. The developer-built recipe (by the cloud LLM that develops this PoC, knowing the internals, from the same six images with no trial limit; not a human) detects 0.98 with 0.08 false alarms and the learned-from-good baseline (memory bank, 20 good images to learn, 10 others for the threshold; not callable through MCP) 0.98 and 0.18. On candles the developer recipe also stays at chance (0.63, 0.73: the wick hole cannot be excluded and the per-image normalisation of the black top-hat reverses the area ranking); the learned baseline gives 0.53 and 0.13. Model names are generic and the LLM endpoint is never recorded. A 72B model was stopped after its first request did not return within 600 s. [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored.py)
  Terms: MCP — An open protocol through which an AI application calls external tools and data: it hands the AI each tool's name, argument schema and results.; LLM — A large neural network trained on text that writes prose or code on request; a smaller one run on one's own computer is called a local LLM.
- [从背景条纹的畸变反推玻璃瓶(仿真):共同假设下的复原,以及无法分开或检测的量](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/01_checker_through_bottle.png) (图): A transparent bottle distorts the stripes behind it. For each pixel we find where the background is seen, fit the bottle geometry and indices backwards, and score 5 fill states against the true values of the simulation. Quantities that are inseparable and errors that stay undetectable are reported too.
  A transparent bottle distorts the stripes behind it. For every pixel the map u says where on the background the ray lands, and radius, wall, n_glass, n_liquid and the fill line are fitted back from it (all in simulation). Two new ops (`watervision`, category `watervision` of `opsoptics`): `bottle_background_map` (the same bottle and camera as `bottle_image`, returning the background coordinate u instead of a brightness) and `bottle_fit_from_pattern` (a fit of the u map returning standard deviations, the correlation matrix, a condition number, the weakest direction and a `separated` flag). Disclosure of the truth and of the shared assumptions: the truth is the input of a simulation made here (R 30 mm, wall 1.5 mm, n_glass 1.52, water 1.333 or oil 1.47, five fills), not a measurement of a real bottle. The observation (a vector-Snell trace) and the forward model of the fit (a closed form on concentric circles) are separate derivations, but both assume the same world: concentric circles with a uniform wall, a horizontal 2-D section, an ideal pinhole, a perfectly flat background, exactly known distances and pixel pitch, no dispersion, a flat liquid surface and point sampling. The result is a recovery under the same assumptions, not evidence that a real bottle can be measured. A paraxial 2x2 ray matrix checks the centre slope (2.2e-5). (1) Without noise all 10 configurations come back to a relative 9.5e-13 and the fill line is exactly on the row boundary; an empty and a full bottle return no fill line. (2) Four-step stripes of periods 256, 32 and 4 mm, shot at 8 bit with 1.5 grey levels of noise, give u back (noise 7.1 um = 0.11 px): R within 1.4e-6, n_liquid within 2.8e-6, fill line 0 px. With 300 noise trials on one row the worst case is 1.8e-5 for R and 1.7e-5 for n_liquid; the sigma predicted from the covariance is 0.67 to 1.00 of the empirical one, so it leans towards an underestimate. (3) The two readings of the fill line (the jump between rows of the stripe map and the backlit brightness) agree to 0 px, but they share the same row split, so this is not an independent measurement; with a 3 mm meniscus the two brightness readings split by 1.15 mm, and the stripe side has no meniscus, so it is unchecked. (4) What cannot be separated: wall and n_glass correlate at -0.92 (cond 11). With the edge pixels even a 0.1 mm wall comes back, but with the usable window narrowed to the middle 25 % the relative sigma of the wall grows 800 times and a 0.1 mm wall does not come back (0.05). The `separated` flag is a rule of thumb for noise-only error, not a guarantee: its threshold was set to 1 % after looking at 1600 trials (at 0.25 it missed 15-20 % of the wrong wall estimates and 73-76 % of the trials with n_glass off by 0.02; at 1 % it missed 0 of the 92 wrong trials of this sweep). With the pattern distance unknown its correlation with n_liquid is -0.999. (5) Precision is not accuracy: noise alone gives n_glass a sigma of 3e-5, but a 1 mm error of the pattern distance moves it by 0.011 (355 times) and a 1 mm error of the camera distance by 0.0029. (6) An error that cannot be detected: an inner wall shifted 0.3 mm (20 % of the wall) along the line of sight in a full bottle (an independently written scalar trace) moves n_glass by -0.095 while the residual stays at the noise floor and converged and separated stay true; in a half-filled bottle the residual grows 8 times and gives it away, but the flag knows nothing of eccentricity. (7) Break-it probes: fixing n_glass at 1.4 raises the residual 414 times and reversing Snell's law 80,000 times. Vertical refraction is not modelled. No learning, no calibration (distances are known), 37 gates. [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse.py)
- [实测 CT:忽略纵向偏移后离参考远 2.6 倍,残差却分辨不出](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/07_geometry_mistakes.png) (图): A walnut was really scanned in a cone beam, and the data were reconstructed with FDK and iterative methods. Using 300 projections and scoring against the reference from its authors, the result that ignores the vertical shift ends up much more distant. But the residual after reprojection cannot reveal the gap.
  A publicly released, measured cone-beam CT scan (Der Sarkissian et al. 2019, one walnut, CC BY 4.0) is reconstructed with Fullseye's scan-mode operators, comparing FDK and three iterative methods (SIRT, OS-SART and total variation, TV) against the reference the providers built from all three of their orbits. It uses 300 of the 1200 projections of the middle orbit (1.2 degree steps), binned 6x6 for the mid-plane and 8x8 for 3-D; a fetch tool pulls only the needed members out of a 6 GB zip with HTTP Range requests (1309 members, pinned by a SHA-256 list). Geometry: read as the circular orbit of `ct_scan_geometry`, the providers' 300 vectors give the SOD and SDD of the scanner's settings file; the two things a circular orbit cannot express (a 0.50 degree in-plane detector roll and a 7.2 pixel vertical offset) are removed by resampling the projections with `warp_parametric`. Mid-plane: FDK on all 300 views scores RMSE 0.0023 /mm and SSIM 0.94 against the reference, and SSIM 0.96 with mean ratio 0.9988 against the providers' FDK (ASTRA, 1200 views). Most of the gap to that FDK's 0.0009 is the coarse grid: rebuilt at 0.1 mm and box-averaged it is 0.0013. At 25 views FDK, SIRT, OS-SART and TV give 0.0126, 0.0074, 0.0067 and 0.0046: TV holds up best, with its weight 1e-05 chosen without the reference, by the reprojection error on views it did not use. With a 90 degree range every method is over four times worse than 300-view FDK (TV 0.0110); TV fills the missing directions with flat patches whose shapes differ from the reference. 3-D: Fullseye's FDK and ASTRA's FDK correlate at 0.9946, and both degrade the same way towards the lower end (0.0026 to 0.0101 for Fullseye, 0.0008 to 0.0099 for ASTRA): a circular orbit does not fully measure the planes away from the mid-plane (Tuy's condition). Geometry mistakes: the adjoint identity <Ax,y> = <x,A^T y> holds to 1e-15 on this scanner's geometry, but it only says the projector pair is matched (mismatching the geometries of A and A^T gives 1.1). Ignoring the vertical offset worsens the RMSE to 0.0060, yet the reprojection residual drops to 0.0850 from 0.0870: a blind spot of the residual. A 0.3 mm lateral shift gives 0.0096 and a reversed rotation 0.0209; the 0.5 degree roll barely matters on a 0.3 mm mid-plane grid. Dual energy: the data hold one energy, so the second energy is a closed-form synthesis, not measured data. Its noise is built from the difference of two real projections taken at the same angle (inside the object the model underestimates the measured noise by a factor 1.25); basis 0 carries 5.06 times the single-energy noise (condition number 15.5). The flat fields are 3.5 times quieter than one projection at the same binning, so noise built from them would be underestimated. [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_walnut_cbct.py)
  Terms: cone-beam CT — A CT geometry in which a point source illuminates a flat-panel detector with a cone of X-rays, so one rotation records a whole volume.; FDK — An approximate cone-beam reconstruction that weights the projections, ramp-filters them row by row and back-projects them; it is exact only in the mid-plane of the orbit.; SIRT — An iterative reconstruction that back-projects the residual of all projections at once and corrects the image step by step, usable where no inversion formula exists.; OS-SART — An iterative method that splits the projections into ordered subsets and applies the SIRT update one subset at a time, which converges faster early on.; total variation — The sum of gradient magnitudes of an image; regularising with it favours piecewise-constant images and fills missing data with flat patches.
- [器材选型地图:用 60 种照明×相机×镜头组合拍同一缺陷](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/02_equipment_at_a_glance.png) (图): We pre-tested visual inspection equipment on the same flaw. With 60 combinations of lighting, camera and lens we mapped how small a flaw is found, with the spread across repeats. It is a synthetic test, not the performance of real equipment.
  Inspection equipment is usually chosen one component at a time, each shown at its best by its own vendor. This synthetic test images the same parts and flaws with six lights (coaxial, dome, 70-degree ring, 30-degree ring, 10-degree dark field, and a phase-shifted fringe screen for deflectometry) x five cameras (monochrome, colour, polarisation, SWIR, hyperspectral) x two lenses (telecentric, ordinary), and reports a90/95 (MIL-HDBK-1823A). Every cell sets its threshold for a 1 % false-call rate on the flaw-free sites it is scored on (a definition for comparing at equal false-call rate; a threshold carried over from training gives 1.1 % pooled and up to 8 % in a single cell). Exposure is capped at 10 ms per field of view (photon counts are not equalised), the coaxial diffuser is twice the field width, and three site seed sets give median and min-max. Machined aluminium scratch parallel to the polishing lines: coaxial light with a telecentric lens is among the best (monochrome 39 um, colour 34 um); dark field with monochrome finds it under every seed set at 76-79 um, and a 30-degree ring with monochrome at 73-74 um at the full budget (at the small CI budget it flips to x with the seeds). Whether bright field needs a telecentric lens depends on the coaxial size: with a diffuser as wide as the field, an ordinary lens detects the largest scratches 80 % of the time (telecentric 100 %); at twice the field an ordinary lens also finds them (92 um, 2.4 times the telecentric value). Dome and high ring confuse the scratch with the polishing lines; the hyperspectral camera's 128 um pixel is too coarse. Shallow paint dent: coaxial, dome and 30-degree ring find nothing with any camera. The fringe screen finds it in all 10 cells (best 21 um) and raking dark field also in all 10 (best 28 um, 1.3 times the fringe value with a cheaper light). The floor is orange peel: cutting its slope from 0.10 to 0.02 degrees takes the fringe a90/95 from 22 to 4.8 um. Raking light wobbles at the small CI budget but settles at the full budget. The dome shows a dent as a bright blob only where its camera hole is mirrored in the slope (60-80 mm from the field centre), so detection moves between 0.33 and 0.92 across the field. The line-scan hyperspectral answer depends on the exposure cap: for the raking-light dent it is 214 um at 10 ms per field (a 30 kHz line rate no real instrument reaches), 16 um at 100 ms, 9.6 um at 1 s, and 9.9 um with the bands simply summed, so it wins on electrons. Black burn speck in resin: found by every set except the fringe screen; a 30-degree ring with a colour camera and ordinary lens (89 um) is enough. White PE fragment in white PP: no visible camera finds it, which is the input (the material table gives both the same visible absorption), not a finding; one SWIR band sees -0.22 % and fails; the 10 nm x 71 band camera finds it under the dome at 678-746 um (about 2 mm under coaxial light). 67 cells hit the exposure cap. Real data (both CC BY 4.0) support only the camera-band axis for polymers, as pixel-level AUC: plastic pieces on pork belly (Ghimpeteanu et al., Zenodo doi:10.5281/zenodo.17242553) go from 0.493 (chance) with one SWIR band to 0.937 / 0.959 / 0.994 with 2 / 6 / 184 bands; PP vs PE fragments (Arbash et al., RODARE doi:10.14278/rodare.3390) reach about 0.63 with monochrome, colour or one SWIR band and 0.882 with 950-1650 nm spectra in 20 nm bins (0.754 when extended to 2231 nm with 110 training pixels). The binning, the window and the real-data gate thresholds were chosen after seeing the data. Lighting, lens and the um values are comparisons inside this synthetic test, not hardware specs; many material values, light levels and price tiers are estimates. a90/95 is the Wald (delta-method) upper bound, and each fit uses 90 flawed sites (MIL-HDBK-1823A recommends at least 40 for a-hat data; the reduced CI run uses 36). The Wald bound is optimistic: in 2000 trials on synthetic data with a known a90, it is at or above the true a90 in 0.89 of fits with 40 sites and 0.92 with 100 (nominal 0.95). A likelihood-ratio bound would be about 4 % larger at the median (39 % or more in the top quarter). The values above are the Wald ones. [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_equipment_selection_map.py)
  Terms: SWIR — The short-wave infrared band, roughly around 1 to 3 µm, where material absorption can distinguish objects that look alike in visible light.; a90/95 — The flaw size at which the lower one-sided 95% confidence bound on the POD reaches 90%.
- [噪声相同,实拍背景上浅伤仍更难看见](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_defect_floor/03_defect_floor_explained.mp4) (视频): Inspection teams often estimate how faint a scratch they can catch on a synthetic image of a flat field plus noise. With the noise level matched, a real brick background raised the detection limit by up to 3.47 times. The limit was set not by noise but by structure in the background, such as mortar joints.
  在实拍背景(砖墙)和噪声量相同的合成背景上,逐渐加深同一个植入缺陷。 **即使噪声量相同,实拍背景的检出极限也是合成背景的 2.03〜3.47 倍(针对位置和振幅已知的缺陷)。** [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_defect_floor.py)
- [用超声波相位找出接合面的空洞](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids/03_amplitude_vs_phase.png) (图): Small voids in the bonding plane of stacked chips are searched for with a scanning acoustic microscope. Every void of 16 um or more appeared inverted and bright in the echo-phase image. In the brightness image many small voids hide among good pixels.
  A thinned 100 um silicon die over a hybrid bond (SiO2 1 + 1 um, Cu pads 1 + 1 um of 40 um diameter at 80 um pitch) with voids of 5 to 24 um placed on pads and in the dielectric, imaged by a focused scanning acoustic microscope (1 mm of water, F/3); echoes come from a layer transfer matrix checked against a bottom-up Airy recursion (Airy multiple-reflection formula), a time-domain ray sum and the optical thin-film formula to 1e-12. Read at the expected time of the bond plane, the dielectric echo is at -91 deg and the Cu pad at +84 deg (thin layers give quadrature echoes) while voids are inverted (-175 deg in the dielectric, +159 deg on a pad). In the amplitude image 8 of 12 voids hide inside the brightness range of intact pixels: an inverted echo adds at right angles to a quadrature background, so voids of 9 to 16 um change the amplitude by 0.4 to 3.5 sigma but the phase-aware statistic by 3.0 to 8.9 sigma; the phase image flags every void of 16 um and up and 1 of 26,724 intact pixels. The conventional single-sample polarity of an intact Cu pad flips 5 times between 140 and 400 MHz. The optimal frequency has a closed form (139.16 MHz for a 10 um void, equal to brute force) and the front-echo tail sets a floor of 130 MHz.  **POD (a-hat vs a, 88 voids): a90/95 of the phase-aware detector is 12.4 / 13.9 / 57.6 um at 150 / 200 / 300 MHz against 16.6 / 19.3 / 101.6 um for amplitude only, under the same baseline and threshold rule. Synthetic: 1-D plane-wave longitudinal layers, a Gaussian lateral PSF, no refraction aberration, silicon attenuation set to 0 (unverified), no kissing bonds.** [源码](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids.py)
  Terms: SAM — An instrument that scans focused ultrasound over a specimen and images internal interfaces or defects from echo amplitude, phase, or arrival time.; Airy — The formula for a parallel plate's transmittance obtained by summing its infinite series of multiple reflections.
- [用深度台阶、V 形缝或彩色分开混载托盘上的纸箱](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/08_depal_robot_video.mp4) (视频): Packed cartons with identical prints are told apart from overhead depth and colour, then unloaded. A depth step splits none, and whether depth dips or dark colour lines win flips across 28 flap-seam builds. With identical prints, deep grooves favour colour; only wide, untaped cuts over shallow grooves favour depth.
  Cartons of different sizes, prints, tape, labels, crush and shrink wrap are stacked two layers high on a JIS T11 pallet and imaged by an overhead RGB-D camera (2.70 m, 960 x 720), 10 pallets for each of 5 conditions (self-made scenes, not external real data). In the base scenes each carton has a 4 mm edge radius and a flap-seam groove along the middle of its top (0.8-2.0 mm, no gap, kraft tape on 70 % and clear tape on 30 %); 40 % of the tops bulge by 3-8 mm. Colour is rendered with Blender Cycles; depth comes from the same geometry through the physics of a pattern-projection sensor. Top faces are segmented with depth_to_organized_points, bilateral_filter_depth, fill_holes, normals_from_depth and watersheds_marker and measured with ransac_plane and fit_rectangle2. Five cues, all classical methods by the same author: depth step / depth V-groove (a step region split along a shallow depth groove, read with crop_rectangle2) / colour only (a weak baseline) / colour groove (a step region split along a dark colour groove) / both (AND). Base-scene first-frame recall (IoU >= 0.5, median of 10 seeds), step / V-groove / colour only / colour groove / both: tight 0.39 / 0.63 / 0.29 / 0.64 / 0.45, identical 0.00 / 0.23 / 0.17 / 0.69 / 0.67, gaps 1.00 / 0.88 / 0.44 / 1.00 / 1.00, shrink 0.44 / 0.32 / 0.35 / 0.50 / 0.48, crushed 0.56 / 0.60 / 0.48 / 0.74 / 0.62. On the unseen seeds 3-9 alone: tight 0.33 / 0.60 / 0.29 / 0.57 / 0.40 (the V-groove slightly ahead), identical 0.00 / 0.17 / 0.17 / 0.71 / 0.67. The V-groove leaves a median 2.5-9.0 false detections per image, the colour groove none. With tight seams the V-groove and the colour groove find about as many faces and the colour groove over-splits less; with identical neighbours the V-groove also splits at the flap seam. Flap-seam map (tight and identical, median of seeds 3-5, all cartons built alike, 28 builds): groove depth 0.2 / 0.5 / 1.0 / 2.0 mm x gap 0 / 0.5 / 1.5 mm x tape (kraft / clear / none). On identical neighbours, under kraft or no tape, the V-groove matches or beats the colour groove when the groove is 1 mm or less (kraft 0.5 mm: 1.00 against 0.83) and collapses at 2 mm, where the colour groove wins (kraft 2 mm: 0.29 against 0.83). With an untaped 1.5 mm gap over a groove of 1 mm or less the colour groove also splits along the dark line of the seam and the V-groove is ahead (1 mm groove: 1.00 against 0.71; 0.2 and 0.5 mm: 1.00 against 0.83; with a 0.5 mm gap they tie). Under clear tape the V-groove drops to 0.57-0.67 even without a gap and the winner varies by cell (0.5 mm gap, 2 mm groove: both 0.29-0.43). With tight seams the differences are small, flipping within 0.57-0.82. The base scenes sat on the deep, gap-free side, which favoured the colour groove. Dependence on the scene: shrinking the edge radius from 4 to 2 to 1 mm takes V-groove recall on identical neighbours from 0.29 to 0.00 to 0.00, and without flap seams and bulges it is 0.83 (numerically rendered, 5 seeds). VALLEY_MIN = 0.5 mm was set after looking at seeds 0-2. The claim gates were set after seeing the results (base scenes on all and on unseen seeds, the map on 3 seeds). The gap-condition floor (>= 0.85) was already there for the step and AND; the colour groove was added after seeing the results, and with the CI stand-in colour (seed 0) it sits at 0.86, a margin of 0.01. Pick-one-and-re-image success (10 seeds, rule-based judgement): tight 0.36 / 0.44 / 0.31 / 0.58 / 0.53, identical 0.12 / 0.28 / 0.23 / 0.53 / 0.44, crushed 0.32 / 0.28 / 0.26 / 0.48 / 0.42, gaps 0.91 / 0.61 / 0.33 / 0.90 / 0.89 (footprint across two cartons = double pick; off the edge, plane residual over 6 mm or peel-moment ratio over 1 = drop; carton on top = snag; carton plus 1.8 kg of tooling above the 12.5 kg payload = overload, which the 11 kg type D exceeds at 12.8 kg). Real-data chapter: on HOPE (real depth from an active-stereo depth camera, 50 val images, 325 faces of box-shaped objects; tabletop toy grocery boxes seen obliquely at 0.5-1 m) the plane-then-rectangle measurement with the same set of operators (segmentation steps and thresholds implemented separately) gives recall 0.705, long-side error median -2.0 mm (absolute median 5.6 mm), normal error 2.6 degrees and 43 false detections per image (background 1395, non-box objects 622, other faces of scored boxes 118, heavily occluded boxes 19). It does not support the headline claim. Credit: Tyree, Tremblay, To, Cheng, Mosier, Smith, Birchfield, IROS 2022 (BOP format). The bundled dataset_info.md says 'Licensed under the CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 license' while the BOP download page says 'license: CC BY-SA 4.0'; we treat it as non-commercial and show numbers only. The video (about 48 s) shows, per condition, one successful and one failed pick by the colour-groove cue with a UR10e (MuJoCo Menagerie model, BSD-3-Clause, plain colours; not affiliated with, endorsed or warranted by any company) on a linear axis driven by inverse kinematics.
